SDL_GameControllerDB is embedded per platform at configure time and handed to SDL through a weak `zn_gamepad_db` symbol, so exports without the generated file still build.** | Browser parity for p5 and Phaser; every existing audio path reused; pads SDL does not know work without a download. | Per-context suspend; sweeps beyond six octaves; a rendered audio graph for p5.sound and Tone.js |
| D220 | What a console core carries; output kept by a host (ZN-1016, ZN-644) | **The plugins a console core links and the host modules a profile has live in `targets/capabilities.json` (`"plugins"`, `"modules"`), read by pack, lint and deploy, not in n3ds/psp/vita entries of each plugin.json (those are build entries of a plugin). A host that keeps the program's output (`zinc mem`, `zinc profile`, the device core) gets `console.error` lines in order, and `sys.exit(n)` ends the run ("exit n"), not the process; every host maps it to the program's code.** | The list belongs to the core; `zinc mem --json` must print only JSON and a device must report the program's exit code. | The core's list duplicated in `targets/console/stubs.cpp` and `player_console.cpp` (have `tools/build-console-core` read capabilities.json) |
| D221 | Lighter idle and exported UI apps (ZN-805) | **An idle window waits in `SDL_WaitEventTimeout`, capped at 100 ms, woken by input, the next due timer (`host.gfxIdleDue`) or a worker message; after an idle wait the 0.1 s `dt` cap is lifted so the JS engines' virtual clocks do not drift. `zinc build` links a lite system host (no Mbed TLS, no llhttp) when the program's host rows use no network or pack writing and it has no plugins and no `zinc:script`, picked by link order, with a fallback to the full host. libuv stays in every program (the loop, the worker pool, the worker wake: 65 KB) and so do SDL3's joystick, gamepad and HIDAPI (gamepads are a feature of every program). The raster band threads start on the first large frame.** | Idle CPU 0.8% → 0.2%, an export 1.1 MB smaller, half the threads with Metal. | Socket events wait up to 100 ms while idle (watch `uv_backend_fd` from a thread, as Electron does); SDL3's render code (614 KB) and the pack writer still linked |
| D222 | Bulk bytes and `zinc:hash` (ZN-1019, ZN-698) | **Byte reads and encodings are one host call on every engine (`host.fsBytes`, `host.sysUtf8Bytes`, `host.sysRandomBytes`), not a loop per byte. `fs.writeBytesAt` opens `r+b`/`w+b` and seeks, never `O_APPEND` (Linux `pwrite` ignores the offset there). `Reply.bytes` is ignored when `file` is set and wins over `body`. SHA-256 lives in its own module `zinc:hash` (`sha256`, `sha256File` in 64 KiB blocks, `toHex`), native on every engine; `zinc:web`'s pure-Zinc `sha256` stays for profiles without host crypto; a program that hashes files links the full system host (`host.hashFile` counts as a crypto row for the lite host).** | 1 MB read 42 ms → 0.3 ms; QuickJS `utf8Encode` was quadratic (58 s → 3 ms); one obvious import for hashing. | A packed `u8[]` (8 bytes per element today), host writes of a `u8[]` still per element; BLAKE2b and an incremental hasher when a caller needs them |
| D223 | CSS sizing in the classic layout; transform origin (ZN-1022, ZN-263.02) | **Classic applies CSS sizing (`flex: 1 1 0%`, a 100% basis that shrinks, the automatic minimum unless `min-w-0`, overflow hidden, a scroll box or `truncate`) only on lines holding a `flex-1`, `w-full` or `h-full` child; every other line keeps classic's rules (no default shrink, grow from the content). The last width or height class wins, as tailwind-merge does. The transform origin is a fraction of the box plus pixels per axis, centre by default; `origin-*` classes and React Native's `transformOrigin` string (lowered to an `origin-[..]` token) set it.** | Tailwind's meaning for the classes people write, Chrome as the reference (19 probe boxes match on three engines), and no pixel moves on lines without these classes; at the default origin the arithmetic is bit-identical. | `flex-1` in a wrapping row still breaks by content width; `shrink`/`flex-auto` shrink to 0; run-time `transformOrigin` values |
| D224 | Function identity, late captures, open records, QuickJS number kinds (ZN-376, ZN-667, ZN-1030, ZN-796) | **A capture-free function used as a value is one object, kept in a lazy global per function (closures still get a fresh object each time, as in JavaScript). A variable captured by a function written above its declaration lives in a cell made at the start of its block. A class shared as an open record gets a real slot for each optional field of that record it lacks (no per-object property map), so an explicit `undefined` reads as absent (`{ b: undefined }` prints `{}`). On QuickJS the typed number wraps (i32/u32 wrap, `Math.imul`, `Math.fround`, conversions on store) take their kinds from each file's syntax, not the checker's types, and i32 `++`/`--` stay as written.** | `f === f` must hold; invalid IR is a crash; slots keep records fast; the QuickJS bundle transforms one file at a time and accepts code the checker refuses. | Fields of other objects and imported signatures on QuickJS (the checker's types in the transform); `{ b: undefined }` printing as JavaScript does |
| D225 | The native host contract (ZN-1069, under D210) | **`"ui": {"render": "drawn" | "native"}` (overridden by `ZINC_RENDER`) selects the renderer; `ui.setHost(commit)` installs a host only under native. After each changed frame the host gets one text buffer of line records (`d` delete, `c` create with kind, `f` frame relative to the parent, `t` text, `p` changed properties, `k` the full ordered child list, `r` roots and layers), deletes first and roots last; a recycled handle is sent as delete then create. Child lists are absolute, not Fabric's index inserts. Native text and field measurement is a C++ hook (`zn::host::setNativeMeasure`) consulted first by the Yoga layout, not a native ABI entry, so ABI v1 stays frozen. The web DOM host shares the hook point and the `dom*` event functions but still reads the tree itself (the browser lays out and needs class rules and states the buffer does not carry).** | One string crosses every engine and the native ABI in one call and reads in tests; lists have no index drift and AppKit's `subviews =` takes a list; drawn programs pay one null check. | A binary record buffer if string building shows in ZN-1074's measures; dom.js reading the buffer |
| D226 | zinc:net in a page; bare import order; workspace packages (ZN-1065) | **zinc:net's fetch in a web page runs through host rows over the page's own `fetch` (`targets/web/rows-net.js`), with host events delivered in every page; servers stay `ZnUnsupported` (W0603) in an export, and zinc:net's event hook is split so a program that only fetches carries no server code. Bare imports resolve as tsconfig `paths`, then a plugin (`module`, `modules` aliases), then `node_modules`, on every target (npm three is reached through `paths`). Workspace packages (a `node_modules` link outside every `node_modules`) are pre-bundled with their files in the key and their folders watched by `zinc dev`.** | One zinc:net module for every engine, dev bridge servers kept; one resolution order everywhere; a workspace edit reloads in 26 ms. | Serving workspace packages unbundled as Vite does (modules swap in place) |
| D227 | Two documentation renderers, two jobs (ZN-1012; refines D176) | **`site/` (Astro) stays the public website for the curated `docs/` guides (`site/docs-map.mjs`, i18n, pagefind). `zinc doc` renders the API and plugin pages (`plugins/<name>/docs/`) and the offline docs a user builds (`--std`, `--serve`); the website links to a `zinc doc --std` build published as a folder of the site at release time, instead of re-rendering plugin and API pages in Astro.** | One source per kind of page, no second renderer for the MDX components. | The 1.6 MB `zinc doc --std` search index too slow on old tablets (then split per section) |
| D228 | How the AppKit host meets the program (ZN-1070, under D210 and D225) | **`import 'zinc:appkit'` installs the host, as React Native macOS needs its package: no per-engine injection, a program without the import is drawn. The plugin reaches the native measure through the C function `zn_host_set_native_measure` by a weak reference (zinc exports no `zn::` C++ symbols; dlsym found nothing in a built AOT app, fixed in ZN-1074). `render: "native"` defaults the layout to Yoga (rn), and only rn leaves are measured natively.** | RN macOS's model (a package, its own views, JS keeps the logic); the fewest engine hooks; dark mode and snapshots for free. |
| D229 | `zinc dev` keeps the optimiser on (ZN-1024) | **The hot recompile of `zinc dev` runs the same optimise passes as `zinc run` and `zinc build`; the save-to-frame time is won by caching unchanged files (parses, scans, JSX lowering, bake scans) and threading the per-function passes (`ir::parallelFor`, at most half the cores, halved under load, `ZN_IR_THREADS`), each step byte-identical to a one-thread cold compile (`tests/t0/hot_recompile.sh`). `zbc::verify` stays on one thread (zbc.cpp is in the ESP32 and console cores, which do not link the IR).** | Skipping optimise saves ~110 ms a save but the dev program would differ from the shipped one: the profiler and frame times would mislead, a Pi would run it slower. Reopen only if incremental compilation is not done and 200 ms is required. |
| D230 | AppKit window chrome and focus (ZN-1071, under D210 and D228) | **Chrome components (Window, Toolbar, ToolbarItem, SplitView, Sidebar) are zinc:ui nodes with a native kind and string attributes (`setNativeKind`/`setNativeAttr`, sent as `c ID KIND` plus `a` records), not new tags: the drawn fallback is the same node with its classes and no target name enters the core. One Yoga tree, no nested roots: the native split only reports the divider, which becomes the first pane's style width (drag on `mouseDragged`, not NSSplitView's modal loop, so frames keep drawing). Keyboard focus stays zinc:ui's (tree order, `tabIndex`, scopes) and the host moves the first responder after each `o ID K` record; no `nextKeyView` chain. | D231 | Records widened are shared; calls through `any` (ZN-1030; amends D215) | **A record or field-only interface widened to an open record shares its original allocation; optional fields get real slots. A dynamic member call captures its function and receiver before arguments; extraction supplies undefined as its receiver. Supported method adapters preserve declaration identity across instances/inheritance, overrides and omitted defaults; JSON omits callable/undefined properties. `unknown` and optional calls retain Z0105. Existing checked representation boundaries remain until shared structural views are implemented.** | Third-pass regression passes on interpreter, AOT and QuickJS. Array aliasing, wider stores, unrelated borrowed receivers and optional-any null versus absence remain explicit gaps in ZN-1030; the checked-store restriction does not satisfy its parity criterion. |
| D232 | AppKit Table and Outline (ZN-1072, under D210, D225, D230) | **A Table's row cache is zinc:ui's own virtual list (`ui.virtualize`: the visible rows plus 3, each marked with the native attribute `row` and read by the host at each commit); the NSTableView data source answers only from that cache and never calls the program, AppKit's scroll comes back as the list's `domScroll`, cells recycle through `makeViewWithIdentifier:`. An Outline gets its whole tree as one attribute, with one AppKit item per id kept across commits (NSOutlineView holds items unretained), and expands and type-selects natively. Reconsider when cells need views (image, checkbox) or trees get too big to send whole. |
| D233 | One connect deadline for zinc:mqtt (ZN-610) | **`MqttClient.connectTimeoutMs` (default 30000, 0 for none) bounds TCP, TLS and the CONNACK as one deadline, like curl's connect timeout and MQTT.js's `connectTimeout`; the error names the phase (`connect timed out`, `TLS handshake timed out`, `no CONNACK from the broker`). The client's lifecycle is one `Phase` enum (Connecting, Securing, Greeting, Connected, Dead). A new row `host.mqttConnect` is appended rather than changing `host.mqttOpenTls` (rows are only added). TLS test brokers are node only. | D234 | Tabular figures ride in the font id (ZN-1062) | **OpenType `tnum` is asked by a name mark on the font, like `~i` (`"Name~t"`), not a per-call flag: one cache key per font, nothing new in the command stream, the GL atlas and every measuring path follow unchanged. | D236 | Plugin views: the registry in the engine (ZN-1075, under D210, D235) | **A spec interface that extends `NativeComponent` (zinc:native) is a native component: its fields are props (string, boolean, number, i32, u32), its `onX` fields events; `zinc native-gen --views` writes the Objective-C++ glue (`Zinc<Kind>View` protocol with `update<Prop>:` setters, so NSControl's own setters are not overridden; an events object; `zinc_views_<x>_register()`). | D237 | Animated's native driver plays a sampled series on the compositor (ZN-364.01, under D182) | **With `useNativeDriver: true` the program samples each animation once (timing, spring, decay; a loop of one) into a value series that the D182 compositor interpolates (`host.gfxLayerSeries`): one implementation of the curves, so values equal the program path exactly (RN's old frames array, generalised). | D238 | Accessible names and edit roles under the AppKit host (ZN-1074, under D225) | **A node's accessible name crosses the host contract as an `a ID axlabel` attribute record (like `placeholder`); the host applies it to the view that takes input (field, text view, table) once a name has been sent. | D239 | Unions of a primitive and an object are held as a Dyn; dead-zone checks resolve scopes (ZN-798) | **A union with two or more non-null members, at least one not an object type and every member convertible to a Dyn (`boolean | MediaTrackConstraints`), is held as a Dyn (`Checker::dynBacked`): its null and undefined are a null reference, it converts on the way in and on narrowed reads or `as` on the way out, and `===` goes through the Dyn helpers. | D240 | `bounds(node)` in zinc:3d (ZN-682) | **The box and sphere of a subtree in the node's parent space (a `Node` has no parent link; for a scene node it is world space). Sphere per mesh centred on its box with the farthest vertex as radius, moved by the matrix, radius times the largest scale, merged as `Sphere.union`. Hidden nodes count, skinned meshes count their bind pose, no precise mode. An empty bounds is +Inf/-Inf with radius -1.** | Cheap enough for every frame, the same numbers as three.js users expect; Ritter or Welzl would be a few percent tighter for more code. |
| D241 | Captures start cleared; test sockets bind port 0; field assignment through branches (ZN-675, ZN-639) | **`grab()` zeroes the capture buffer it allocates (once, not a clear per frame in `render_rows`): every capture path (`ZINC_SHOT`, `zinc capture`, `ZINC_FRAMEHASH`, DevTools, zbench) hashes the same frame each run, as Skia surfaces and a new HTML canvas start transparent black. | D244 | `zinc assets check`, `report --diff`, `clean` (ZN-437) | **`check` builds the release packs for the target, then reads the `report.json` the build wrote (sizes are measured once, by the build; `zn::assets::readReport` only adds them up per group). `download` and `flash` budgets cover every pack; `boot` the groups of `loading.groups` (else `boot`), each counting the larger of pack and decoded bytes; `ram` and `gpu` each group alone (a group is loaded whole), so a streaming game does not fail on its total. | D246 | The real DevTools frontend drives Zinc's CDP (ZN-709, ZN-704) | **Checks drive Chrome's own DevTools frontend through its UI (CDP `Input` mouse and keys, no test hooks; only `captureScreenshot` over its connection to compare the app's frame): `tests/devtools_frontend_check.py sources|styles`. A CDP debug session has one `Phase` (Waiting, Running, Paused) changed by `to()`; paused-only requests that arrive while running are refused with V8's message, not queued; call frame and object ids carry the pause generation so an id from an earlier pause is refused; disconnect resets enabled and auto-attach; `Debugger.enable` on a paused program sends `Debugger.paused` again. | D248 | zinc:net bodies: 16 MiB default, streamed uploads, our own sendfile (ZN-1028) | **`maxBodyBytes` defaults to 16 MiB (32 connections × 16 MiB = half a Pi 3); the 413 and 431 replies carry a text naming the limit and the option, and the program warns once per server. Plain-http file replies call `sendfile(2)` directly on macOS and Linux (libuv's `uv_fs_sendfile` tries `copy_file_range` first and then blocks in `poll()` on a socket; off on iOS where it is SIGSYS); never under TLS, whose copy path no longer loads the whole file for a fast client. `serve` honours `ZINC_BIND` when no `hostname` is given and does not listen dual-stack by default; serve and fetch ignore SIGPIPE unless the program set a handler.** | Flat memory for large files on a 1 GB board (1631 → 29 MB upload, 572 → 21 MB server) and twice the download speed. |

| D250 | Layered and inset shadows; simpler borders on T0 (ZN-260, ZN-257, under D245) | **`shadow-[...]` takes CSS box-shadow layers (offset, blur, spread that grows box and radius, colour, `inset`; `_` for spaces) and replaces the preset; Tailwind v4 `inset-shadow-*` and a style object's `boxShadow` lower to it. The engine reuses the plugin's vendored miniaudio and qoa.h in one C file compiled with hidden visibility.** IMA ADPCM encoding later. | 4.95x smaller than PCM, ~25 KB streamed, no symbol clash with the plugin. |
| D255 | zinc dev web: a hardened bridge and a wasm engine (ZN-1033, ZN-863.06, after D242) | **The dev bridge listens on 127.0.0.1, answers only the dev page's origin, refuses dot segments, carries bytes as base64, and is one enum `Dev::Bridge {Off, Fresh, Used, Ended}` with a token per page load (an older tab gets 403). `zinc dev --target web --engine wasm` reuses `zinc export --target wasm` and its SharedArrayBuffer glue served with COOP/COEP, not the playground's JSPI worker (JSPI is Chrome/Edge 137+ only); a save reloads the whole page (a fresh worker is the only clean restart of a wasm instance); its build is `WasmDev::Build {Building, Ready, Failed}`. | D256 | Font importer and MSDF text in the core (ZN-442, ZN-674) | **The `font` importer subsets with hb-subset to named charsets and the characters of asset files (side files in the cache key). `"sdf": "msdf"` bakes an rgba8 atlas with msdfgen 1.13 core (vendored, build side only, outlines from HarfBuzz, no FreeType), range fixed at 4 atlas pixels in `include/zn/msdf.h`. MSDF is core, not a plugin, because a plugin cannot add a sampling mode: `ImageFlag.Msdf` (IMAGE grad 3) on the raster and the GL replay, the screen range computed on the CPU so GLES 2 needs no derivatives; `zinc:gfx/msdf` draws one `drawImageRect` per glyph. `imageFromBytes` decodes GIF's first frame (stb_image; the animation path with its known bugs stays unused).** | Sharp text at any scale for one atlas; +7 KB for GIF. |
| D257 | QuickJS typed fast calls for the host (ZN-596, ZN-601) | **Patch 0004 adds `JS_NewCFunctionFast` (a typed C function called from `OP_call`/`OP_call_method` when every argument tag matches, else its generic twin) and the non-throwing accessors `JS_GetBufferData`/`JS_GetFastArray`. Every `__host_*` row of only d/i/u/b letters is a fast function (not exit, flushing rows or workers); a fast function never allocates, throws or calls JS. Node-API bindings (libzn_webgl, plugins) stay generic. Binding rules in `docs/plugins.md`, checked by a T0; `tools/bench --gate` fails a case 30% slower than `baseline.json`. | D258 | iPod lists break ties by title (ZN-1049) | **Recommendations (genius, forYou, moods, radio) order equal scores by title, then by id: iPod track ids are random per device, so lists are the same on every iPod with the same songs.** | Stable goldens on any machine. |
| D259 | zinc:3d World shadows are one renderer state (ZN-1047) | **`ShadowState {None, Hidden, Shown}` changed by one transition (`shadowsTo`), each entry under one RAII lock (the PSP sweep semaphore, never nested). Hiding keeps the heights and frees only the drawn map: on the 3DS after the GPU frame (`later()`), on the PSP under the sweep lock; closing the World drops them. `World.setShadows(on)` and `shadowsState`. glTF night conventions stay the material extras `night` and `glow`: no standard exists and `KHR_materials_variants` swaps whole materials instead of blending.** | Tokyo reads `hmap.bin` once; no free while a frame may draw. |
| D260 | Pictures decode off the frame; createImageBitmap in zinc:script WebGL (ZN-569) | **One process-wide decode thread, one completion queue per context (`Open`/`Closed`); ids never reused; a cancelled, stale or closed request is dropped when its result arrives; a decode is never interrupted; bytes are lent, and the queue waits for them before it closes. `createImageBitmap` exists in zinc:script WebGL contexts (three.js GLTFLoader needs it there) but stays out of the web environment until a Worker exists (ZN-587): PixiJS 7 and 8 load no picture when it exists without a Worker. A bitmap keeps its decoded pixels; its flipY and premultiply flags apply at upload and win over the context's.** | A 4096² PNG no longer stalls a frame (300 ms → 20 ms gaps). |
| D261 | The museum table fits its cards above the button rows by scaling (ZN-1040) | **At the larger text sizes the designed layout is scaled about the middle of the far edge by (edge − 12) / lowest card, never below 0.5, only when the rows overlap, so the designed size stays pixel identical.** Rejected: reflowing the cards (changes the author's composition), shrinking the rows (buttons must stay finger-sized). An optional chain now short-circuits as a whole on every engine (`a?.b.c(x)`, `m?.get(k) ?? d`). | Accessible sizes without touching the author's layout. |
| D262 | Math gives the same bits on every CPU, libc and engine (ZN-703) | **Math.sin/cos/tan/exp/log/atan2 run V8 13.6's fdlibm (vendored unmodified, `third_party/ieee754`) compiled without FMA contraction; Math.pow and `**` apply V8's rules first (NaN exponent, (±1)**±Infinity, x**2, x**0.5) then CORE-MATH's correctly rounded pow. One C entry per function (`include/zn/jsmath.h`), the interpreter and AOT through ops.h, QuickJS through patch 0005. Equals Node 24 x86_64; Node arm64 is not bit-reproducible (FMA) and Apple's pow misrounds 13 of 10,000.** Rejected: the platform libm, fdlibm's pow (795 of 10,000 differ), glibc's trig (LGPL), FMA contraction. | One golden on every machine; a few ns per call (indicative). |
| D263 | Object statics on records and any values (ZN-161.01) | **On typed objects `Object.assign/getOwnPropertyNames/defineProperty` lower from the static types (assign into `{}` is a spread); `Object.freeze` of a typed object returns it unchanged (its type says what may change); `defineProperty` only sets a declared field; `fromEntries` returns an `any` object. On `any` values a Dyn prelude keeps frozen, read-only, hidden keys and key order in `DynObj.attrs`, so JSON, console.log and for...in skip hidden keys with no runtime change.** Rejected: a frozen bit on every object, a fromEntries returning a Map. | Node's output on three engines; unused statics cost nothing. |
| D264 | site/ and zinc doc keep different jobs (ZN-1012, refines D176) | **site/ (Astro) stays the public website for the curated guides (learn, start, guide, reference, plugins/*.md, targets) with i18n and pagefind search; zinc doc makes the API and plugin pages (plugins/<name>/docs, api.html) and the offline docs (`--std`, `--serve`); the website links to a `zinc doc --std` build published as a folder of the site at release, instead of Astro re-rendering them. `zinc doc` links a project's source files to the `repository` of zinc.json (`<repo>/blob/main/<path>`). The `--std` search index (1.6 MB, 302 KB gzipped) stays one deferred file: it works from file:// and is cached after the first page; a project's is tens of KB.** | One source and one renderer per kind of page. |
| D265 | AOT resources assembled with `.incbin` (ZN-429) | **AOT resources are assembled by `.incbin` from a file beside the generated C++ (`<out>.cpp.res`), through one top-level asm block; the C++ name is bound by an asm label (`zn_aot_resources`).** Sections: Mach-O `.const_data`, ELF `.rodata` with `%object`/`.size`, COFF `.rdata`; `.p2align 4`, a trailing NUL. ## D279 — Unauthenticated packs execute source on QuickJS (ZN-445)

QuickJS bytecode is trusted executable input. [QuickJS-ng's developer guide](https://quickjs-ng.github.io/quickjs/developer-guide/intro/) says the bytecode reader does not validate untrusted data. A pack's SHA-256 inventory checks corruption; a sender can replace both data and hashes, so it does not authenticate bytecode. Keep the version/commit-stamped local cache and writer, retain original pack sources, and disable bytecode reads for unsigned unpacked/fused programs. A visible warning explains the source fallback; incompatible local cache entries explain how to rebuild. Native plugin trust flags keep their existing meaning.

Security validation is a hard constraint: unauthenticated bytecode loading is ineligible regardless of speed. Source fallback reuses existing compilation without a signing service or format. Exact tests prove stale/truncated local cache rejection and hostile pack bytecode rejection even after inventory hashes are recomputed; real Fatty direct and packed source runs pass. Revisit packed bytecode acceleration only when provenance is authenticated, with explicit compatibility and trust-boundary tests. Global CLI flags currently miss the early `.zapp` route and fail rather than loading its bytecode; a future route repair must carry provenance from successful unpacking, not infer trust from a filename. Integrated source: `5acf53be0`; quiet startup timings remain owner-picked.

## D280 — Schedule QuickJS cycle scans without claiming a pause bound (ZN-575)

Keep the pinned QuickJS collector. [QuickJS-ng internals](https://quickjs-ng.github.io/quickjs/developer-guide/internals/) and its public API expose whole-heap `JS_RunGC`, not an interruptible collection budget. At main gfx frame boundaries, use constant-time allocator growth and the preceding pause estimate to choose when to start a scan. At +8 MiB, start when the estimate fits remaining 16.7 ms; at +32 MiB, start regardless. QuickJS's automatic threshold remains a pressure fallback inside a callback. Initial loading gets 16 MiB headroom; RAII restores the prior threshold when the frame loop exits. These are heuristics, neither a hard memory cap nor a real-time guarantee.

The only vendored change exposes the existing allocator byte counter, through the pinned reversible patch queue. Existing frame phase IDs stay unchanged, with `gc` appended as ID9. Text, JSON, trace, Info and HUD work totals include boundary collection time. Loading, emergency, worker and embedded-script scans remain in their actual triggering phase or outside the frame profile; the new phase does not pretend to observe all collection.

Scores in RULES order: automatic-only 2/2/5/5/5/5/5 =52; boundary scheduling 4/3/4/4/5/5/4 =56; incremental collector fork 5/4/1/1/5/3/1 =45. Performance scores are expectations. Exact policy/lifecycle/profile/cache tests and unchanged Fatty pass on the combined binary; no latency or startup measurements were run. The original GC probe has synchronous top-level loops and Phaser/Babylon probes use `loadScript`, so neither the4ms criterion nor cache/evaluation thresholds follow from frame-boundary proof. Revisit after matched quiet measurements, keeping full collection time in frame work. ZN-575 remains Review with AC1/AC3 open. Integrated `43af3d75d`; root proof `.logs/integration-2026-11-10/gc-nested-smoke.log`.

## D281 — Update-enabled desktop exports reuse their hosted engine (ZN-803)

Use the existing pack/fuse and hosted engine for desktop applications declaring an update public key. This preserves the asynchronous signed updater and caller validation before atomic staging, including warm source launches. Ordinary native exports retain their existing AOT path. A raw AOT updater reports the supported export/fuse route; it does not silently lose the service. macOS bundles seal app.zapp in Resources, retaining normal signing and bundle metadata.

The hosted export is larger; no startup comparison is claimed. Museum keeps its own content validator, while generic update-app verifies package/program trust and identity. A normal quit joins a pending bounded operation and can wait up to135 seconds of network time plus local checks; cancellation is deferred. Actual source, packed, fused and signed.app update/trial-promotion proofs are recorded in the task. Revisit a raw-AOT bridge only for a concrete caller that needs it. Integrated3a87af69a.


## D282 — Browser workers reuse the typed worker bridge (ZN-587)

One existing worker thread, QuickJS runtime, event pump and queue path serves typed zinc:worker and the web facade. A separate owned source payload identifies classic/module and file/Blob source, leaving the inspector URL short. No source tempfile or second registry is needed. Structured clone preserves references; clone/transfer errors leave buffers attached, accepted serialization detaches them even when the target has terminated. Each queue accepts at most256 messages and16 MiB; pop/termination/join release accounting and explicit overflow refuses the post. These are bounded copies, not zero-copy transfer.

The worker context reuses existing file/network permissions and image decoding without a DOM, drawing surface or native plugin registry. Permission configuration/warnings and filesystem scopes/grants now use coherent provider locks, always scope before permission. Actual pinned Pixi8.22 texture loading returns the expected32×24 pixels; Tone15.1.22 uses its worker ticker. Typed worker messages/crash handling remain intact. Local file/Blob scripts are supported; network scripts, nested workers, importScripts, MessagePort/OffscreenCanvas transfer and worker-global interception of uncaught errors remain outside the contract. Exact concurrency/clone/lifecycle checks pass; no TSAN, hardware, long churn or performance claim. Integrated97af370e3.

Borrowed methods using scalar/Dyn properties run against their actual receiver through lazily generated helpers, with declaration identity, defaults and lexical module bindings preserved. The pristine method cache belongs only to the checker and unused copies never enter emitted AST. Typed structured/array/getter/private/super/generic cases keep their safe checked path; structural arrays and wider stores remain unfinished. Type annotations use the type namespace independently of value bindings; the original unknown-valued fixture is restored. Integrated44457803a and4f198e9c1, focused all3 regression passes.

## D283 — Share AOT declarations and losslessly pack native embedded data (ZN-603)

Large generated programs share one declarations header, with its content hash in every translation unit cache key. Copy back only a successful non-void call result, preserving explicit RC and exceptional flow. Native graphics builds reuse the existing zstd decoder for resources and metadata when smaller; compiled original sizes bound decompression and both frame/result sizes must match. No new codec, wire format, removed feature or externalized asset buys the size reduction.

Scores in RULES order: existing zstd path5/4/5/4/5/4/4 =63/70; RLE only2/5/2/5/5/5/5 =55/70. These are design judgements. Raw stdout, cross/console/mobile exports, .app and --no-embed routes retain their previous representation. Audited Lottie/video use the existing lite-host contract; network rows in nested typed workers and undecodable workers still select the full host. Resources are copied by the existing installer, then their temporary ends; the418,786-byte table buffer remains through runProgramPacked. Revisit for a constrained native target, compile budget regression or future code growth.

## D285 — Never is distinct from null; nonreturning calls reuse existing IR (ZN-1120)

Append a distinct frontend Never without shifting fixed primitive IDs0–6. A never-returning function has an existing void IR return; a known-never call preserves exceptional unwind and ends only its normal continuation with Unreachable. Valued closure and virtual signatures use existing adapters ending Unreachable, not invented return values. A demanded unreachable SSA id lives only in a predecessor-free dead block removed by existing cleanup.

Uninhabited storage uses a lazy private empty __Never REF class, separate from __Void, with no constructor, instances or source binding. Programs needing only throwing calls emit no carrier. Assertions/projections that would materialize a never value diagnose their current unsupported boundary. Real null is not erased to void; existing standalone null/undefined and catch(void) generic gaps remain separate.

Scores in RULES order: distinct frontend type plus existing IR5/4/5/4/5/5/4 =64/70; new bottom opcode/runtime representation5/3/3/2/5/2/1 =47/70. These are design judgements; aliasing null is ineligible because it accepts invalid returns and breaks real null signatures. Exact all3 checks cover direct/closure/virtual/base/interface/fewer-argument calls, exceptions/finally, typed joins, generic rejection paths and eleven invalid cases. No VM, AOT emitter, ABI or opcode change. Integrated903f17ed4; root exact Never and original record/namespace regressions pass on07ed10/5b9fea69. Revisit inhabited projections and real null representation as their own coherent extension.

## D286 — Alternative engines share export finalization and verified portable packs (ZN-1066)

## D288 — Query the last rendered depth for 3D marker visibility (ZN-683)

Camera.isVisible projects world coordinates with its target-owned saved matrix and compares the last rendered depth. This handles actual concave/transformed/deformed geometry without retaining a second mesh copy. Software reads existing depth; macOS OpenGL resolves and reads one pixel. RAII restores context and framebuffer state. Targets add one64B projection snapshot. Invalid finite/bias inputs reject, and unsupported GPU backends report their limitation.

[OpenGL depth readback](https://registry.khronos.org/OpenGL-Refpages/gl4/html/glReadPixels.xhtml), [multisample blits](https://registry.khronos.org/OpenGL-Refpages/gl4/html/glBlitFramebuffer.xhtml) and [three.js Raycaster](https://threejs.org/docs/pages/Raycaster) support the tradeoff. A synchronous query may stall; blended geometry without depth writes does not occlude, and async colour presentation can lag this depth by one frame. Revisit for many queries per frame or other GPU backends.

## D309 — Initialize WebGL storage explicitly and clip source copies to the specification (ZN-701)

Use bounded row staging for texture zeros and typed scratch-FBO renderbuffer clears, including multisampling. ## D311 — Linux loads the desktop window host through the existing module boundary (ZN-592.04)

Reuse the macOS loader and static AOT window archive. Desktop GNU Linux defaults to a PIC shared module containing SDL; musl stays static. Export SDL globally for later graphics/plugins, bind only internal boundary functions locally, and keep runtime data shared. Zig0.15 rejects Bsymbolic-functions, so its module marks the existing boundary declarations protected; other linkers use Bsymbolic-functions. No HAL/driver or runtime ABI change.

Weighted alternatives (fit/performance/size/maintainability/licence/portability/effort, weights3/3/2/2/2/1/1): keep static SDL 3/2/1/4/5/4/5=44; existing lazy boundary 5/4/5/4/5/4/3=62. This selects separation, not a measured startup-speed claim. [dlopen](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man3/dlopen.3.html), [GNU linker](https://sourceware.org/binutils/docs/ld/Options.html), [SDL CMake](https://wiki.libsdl.org/SDL3/README-cmake).

Loader states distinguish Loading/Ready/Failed with locked reentry handling. Fused extraction verifies actual trusted embedded bytes under FileLock and rechecks before dlopen; packaging carries module dependencies.
